# Synergy report (S36): tables

Full study batch: studies B (500 adults) and B10 (10,000 adults), seeds 1-500, engine `ce8d030`, the frozen engine on every row. Generated by `python3 dev/tools/s36_synergy.py <compassionism-sim-results checkout> dev/runs/s36-synergy` from BetterToBest/compassionism-sim-results `prog-b-ce8d030` and `prog-b10-ce8d030`. Every interval is a 95% paired-bootstrap interval over seeds (10,000 replicates). v(S) is the effect of the cell with the parts in S on, against no programme; for the share outcomes a reduction is a positive number of percentage points, so a positive contrast is synergy (the parts together cut the share by more than their separate effects add up to). For cost, a positive contrast means the parts together cost more than separately. For inflation, positive means more inflation together. Flags: † interval excludes zero; M also material (1 point; 5% of the full design's cost; 1 point of the yearly inflation rate); H survives Holm over the 11 contrasts of its row. Cumulative inflation compounds, so its contrasts are not judged material (Amendment 9); the yearly-rate row is. The design's own BLEI is shown, never claimed. Every estimate is also in `estimates.csv`.

## 0. Checks

- PASS  B: the none row and the 192 rows without CCO are identical under the two designs (every key but the C2.2 design's own reporting keys, null on the release design; every seed): 0 differ
- PASS  B10: the none row and the 48 rows without CCO are identical under the two designs (every key but the C2.2 design's own reporting keys, null on the release design; every seed): 0 differ
- PASS  B: in all 576 40-year rows, the year-20 measures (wealth, price level, BLEI and cost-of-living shares at year 20) equal the 20-year rows seed by seed: 0 of 2304 differ
- PASS  B against study A: the 72 rows at 500 adults that both hold (none, full design, raise on; both designs, yardsticks, horizons, environments) are identical, every key and seed: 0 differ
- PASS  rows per design: B 576/576 (576 = 96 per environment and horizon x 3 x 2), B10 96/96 (96 = 32 x 3)
- PASS  Shapley values add up to v(all five) (efficiency), as linear maps
- PASS  the 26 dividends plus the five single-part effects add up to v(all five)
- PASS  the whole-design contrast equals the sum of the 26 dividends
- PASS  per seed, in every setting and outcome, the five Shapley values add up to v(all five) (largest relative gap 9.1e-16)

## 7. Scale: 10,000 adults against 500 (Q1, decision rule 3, contrast by contrast)

Independent runs. A difference passes the scale rule where |Delta| exceeds both its Monte Carlo error (the half-width of its interval) and the materiality threshold (1 point; 5% of the 500-adult full cost; 1 point of the yearly inflation rate).
